Num 36:13 [אֵלֶּה] [הַמִּצְוֹת] [וְהַמִּשְָּׁטִים] [אֲשֶׁר] [צִוָּה] YHWH/[the] LORD [בְּיַד] [מֹשֶׁה] to/toward [בְּנֵי] [יִשְׂרָאֵל] [בְּעַרְבֹת] Moab upon [יַרְדֵּן] [יְרֵחוֹ] | [KJV:] These are the commandments and the judgments, which the Lord commanded by the hand of Moses unto the children of Israel in the plains of Moab by Jordan near Jericho.
